Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a seismology algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 44. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 907/2.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to seismology theory, we decompose the problem into 44 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 907. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on seismology structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 466. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 63 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on seismology structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 466. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 63. QED.
